```
def project_feasible(h,n):
  target=n/2
  h=np.clip(h,0,1)
  for _ in range(100):
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
    if diff>0:
      room=1-h; tot=room.sum()
      if tot<=0: break
      step=diff/tot
      # need avoid overshoot? h += step*room; clip; actual may be less. Loop.
    else:
      tot=h.sum(); step=diff/tot; h += step*h; clip
  # final exact adjust at fractional coords
  diff=target-h.sum()
  if abs(diff)>1e-12:
    idx=np.where((h>1e-12)&(h<1-1e-12))[0]
    if len(idx)==0: ...
    # distribute diff over idx by small increments
```

def coordinate_descent(h,n,init_c, passes, deadline, prep, random_order=False, rng):
  c=init_c
  for it in range(passes):
    if time.time()>deadline: break
    order = list(range(n)); if random: rng.shuffle(order)
    improved=False
    for p in order:
       h0=h[p]
       x=solve_coordinate(...)
       if abs(x-h0)>1e-10:
          update; improved=True
    # project feasible
    h2=project_feasible(h,n)
    if norm(h2-h)>small:
       h=h2; recompute c
    else:
       # maybe recompute c to correct drift?
       c=correlate... 
    if not improved: break
    # if c max no improve for some passes, stop
  return h,cmax
```

Let's define search schedule concretely:
```
def run(seed,budget_s):
  t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-5
  rng=RandomState(seed)
  best_h,best_c,n_best = load_parent or default
  # Local refine main parent
  best_h,best_c = refine(best_h,n_best, deadline-500)
  # Candidate list: parent plus interpolated n
  ns = unique nearby even/odd: n_best, n_best-100, -50, +50, +100, maybe int(0.7*n), 1.3*n up to 1000.
  for n_try in ns:
    if time > deadline-150: break
    h_try = interp(parent to n_try); project
    # If n_try <= 900, refine with proportional time
    dur = min(60, remaining*0.3)
    h_try,c_try = refine(h_try,n_try,dur)
    if c_try < best_c - 1e-12: update
  # Random trig/binary starts for small n (maybe 350-800)
  for k in range(... while time):
    h_rand = trig_start(n_small, rng) or binary_start(...)
    h_rand,c_rand = refine(h_rand,n_small, step=10-20s)
    update
  # Perturb best and refine
  for sigma in [0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2]:
    while time:
      hp = project(best_h + noise); binary occasionally
      hp,cp = refine(hp,n_best, dur=30)
      if cp<best: update
  # final intense CD on best remaining time
  final refine(best_h,n_best, remaining)
  return project(best_h), compute_c5, len
```

Using top-K subgradient of max (not smooth) may be too aggressive; use smoothed weights over top 100 with beta=200/exp? Let's define weights:
```
def active_weights(c, k=80):
  m=c.max()
  idx=np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
  sc=c[idx] - m # <=0
  z=np.exp(50*sc)  # beta 50
  w=z/z.sum()
  return idx,w
```
Gradient as sparse top.
This may reduce C. But line search with exact C and projection could be okay. It evaluates c each attempt (np.correlate). Could be many. Use step initial 0.02. Maybe 100 iterations.
Need implement gradient sparse correctly with output index weights. Let's write `subgradient(c,h,n,k=40, beta=200)`:
```
idx = np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
vals = c[idx]
z = np.exp(beta*(vals - vals.max()))
w = z/z.sum()
g = np.zeros(n)
for idx,val,w in zip(idx, vals, w):
  s = int(idx) - (n-1)
  # first + (1-h[p-s])
  if s >=0:
    p0, p1 = s, n
    i0, i1 = 0, n-s
  else:
    p0, p1 = 0, n+s
    i0, i1 = -s, n
  if p1>p0: g[p0:p1] += w*(1 - h[i0:i1])
  # second - h[p+s]
  if s >=0:
    p0, p1 = 0, n-s
    j0, j1 = s, n
  else:
    p0, p1 = s+n, n
    j0, j1 = 0, n+s
  if p1>p0: g[p0:p1] -= w*h[j0:j1]
return g
```
Check indices for first: term + (1-h_{p-s}) for p=i+s. If s>=0: i 0..n-1-s, p s..n-1. If s<0: i -s..n-1, p 0..n-1+s. Good. Second: -h_{p+s} for i=p, j=p+s valid. If s>=0: p 0..n-1-s, j s..n-1. If s<0: p -s..n-1, j 0..n-1+s. Good. At s=0, first add 1-h[0:n], second -h[0:n], okay.

def interpolate_h(old,n_new):
  if len(old)==n_new: return old.copy()
  x_old=np.linspace(0,2,len(old),endpoint=False)
  x_new=np.linspace(0,2,n_new,endpoint=False)
  return project_feasible(np.interp(x_new,x_old,old),n_new)

Let's detail coordinate_descent code with prep.
```
def coordinate_descent(h, n, time_limit, fast=False):
  t_end = time.time() + time_limit
  h = project_feasible(h.copy(), n)
  c = c_array(h,n)
  best_h = h.copy(); best_c = float(np.max(c))
  prep = prepare_indices(n,bits?)
  bisections = 22 if fast else 32
  passes_max = 1000
  for pass in range(passes_max):
    if time.time() >= t_end: break
    order = np.arange(n)
    if pass % 2: order = order[::-1]
    # maybe shuffle every 3 passes
    improved = False
    start=time.time()
    for step,p in enumerate(order):
       if step % 100 ==0 and time.time()>=t_end: break
       x = solve_coord(h,c,p,n,prep,bisections)
       if abs(x-h[p])>1e-10:
          update...
          improved=True
    h = project_feasible(h,n)
    c = c_array(h,n)
    cur=max(c)
    if cur < best_c - 1e-12:
       best_c=cur; best_h=h.copy()
    if not improved: break
    if time.time() >= t_end: break
  return best_h,best_c
```

def block_start(n,rng):
  k=rng.randint(3,15)
  h=np.zeros(n); pos=0; val=rng.randint(2)

Pseudo:
```
def c_array(h,n): return np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full') * (2.0/n)

def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
    target=0.5*n
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    for _ in range(200):
        h=np.clip(h,0,1)
        diff=target-h.sum()
        if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
        if diff>0:
            room=1.0-h
            tot=room.sum()
            if tot<1e-15: break
            h = h + (diff/tot)*room
        else:
            tot=h.sum()
            if tot<1e-15: break
            h = h + (diff/tot)*h
    # exact adjust residual on fractional
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    diff=target-h.sum()
    while abs(diff)>1e-12:
       idx=np.where((h>1e-12)&(h<1-1e-12))[0]
       if len(idx)==0:
           # choose any with room
           if diff>0: idx=np.where(h<1)[0]
           else: idx=np.where(h>0)[0]
           # adjust one by one
           i=idx[0]
           step=np.clip(diff, -h[i], 1-h[i])
           h[i]+=step; diff-=step; continue
       # distribute over idx proportional
       if diff>0:
          room=1-h[idx]
          # cap
          steps=np.minimum(room, diff/len(idx)* (room+1e-30)/max(room.sum(),1e-30)) # complicated
       simpler loop over idx:
       for i in idx:
          step=np.clip(diff, -h[i], 1-h[i])
          h[i]+=step; diff-=step
          if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
    return h
```
This exact adjustment loops over n maybe okay. Need avoid long loop if diff small. Could use iterative proportional enough. But final exact sum within 1e-9 accepted. Use 500 loops of proportional distribution; empirical sum error tiny. The previous 5000 loop okay. Use 2000.

For performance, project called many times. Need faster. Use a simpler projection that often exact in one go:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
  target=n/2
  h=np.clip(h,0,1)
  for _ in range(100):
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)<1e-11: break
    if diff>0:
      room=1-h; s=room.sum()
      if s==0: break
      h += diff/s * room
    else:
      s=h.sum()
      if s==0: break
      h += diff/s * h
  h=np.clip(h,0,1)
  # If residual, adjust smallest fractional
  diff=target-h.sum()
  if abs(diff)>1e-12:
    frac=np.where((h>1e-9)&(h<1-1e-9))[0]
    if len(frac)>0:
      order=frac[np.argsort(np.minimum(h[frac],1-h[frac]))]
      for i in order:
        step=np.clip(diff, -h[i], 1-h[i])
        h[i]+=step; diff-=step
        if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
  return h
```
If many h at bounds and no room, can't satisfy target unless initial impossible; but parent/project from feasible, so okay.

Coordinate prep:
```
def prepare_indices(n):
  ms=[]; jf=[]; jb=[]
  for p in range(n):
    ms.append(np.arange(n-1-p, 2*n-1-p, dtype=np.int32))
    jf.append(p + ms[-1] - (n-1))  # int maybe negative? if int32 okay? p+ms-(n-1) may be negative; int32 handles.
    jb.append(p - ms[-1] + (n-1))
  return ms,jf,jb
```
But creating all arrays for n=1000 takes 0.? memory. Use tuple.

`solve_coord(c,h,p,n,prep, step, bis)`:
```

h0=h[p]
a=np.zeros(n, float)
vf=(jf>=0)&(jf<n)
if vf.any(): a[vf] += 1.0 - h[jf[vf]]
vb=(jb>=0)&(jb<n)
if vb.any(): a[vb] -= h[jb[vb]]
diag = p  # index in ms where m=n-1
a_diag = a[diag]  # should 1-2h0 if both valid? Check when p any, ms includes n-1; jf=p, jb=p, vf/vb true, a=np.add then subtract =1-2h0. Good.
a_aff = a if diag not? We'll create a_aff = a.copy(); a_aff[diag]=0.0
dom_lo=max(0,h0-step); dom_hi=min(1,h0+step)
bc = c[ms] - a_aff*h0
lb = c[ms].copy()? Could compute for comb: For aff_mask:
aff = np.ones(n,bool); aff[diag]=False

```
def active_weights(c,k=60,beta=100.0):
 if len<=k: idx=np.arange(len); vals=c
 else: idx=np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
 vals=c[idx]; m=vals.max(); z=np.exp(beta*(vals-m)); w=z/z.sum(); return idx,w
```
Gradient:
```
def subgrad_scaled(h,n,c,k,beta):
 idx,w=active_weights(c,k,beta)
 g=np.zeros(n)
 dx=2.0/n
 for ii,ww in zip(idx,w):
   s=int(ii)-(n-1)
   if s>=0:
     # first
     if n-s>0: g[s:n] += ww*(1.0 - h[0:n-s])
     # second
     j=s:n; g[0:n-s] -= ww*h[j]
   else:
     t=-s
     # first p 0:n+t, i t:n
     g[0:n+t] += ww*(1.0 - h[t:n])
     # second p t:n, j 0:n+t
     g[t:n] -= ww*h[0:n+t]
 return dx*g? If c is scaled, weights of scaled c, gradient scaled = dx*coeff. But if c scaled, c = raw*dx; weights same; derivative of c_scaled = dx*coef. So yes return dx*g.
```

Let's adjust `prepare_indices` accordingly:
```
def prepare_indices(n):
  ms_list=[]; jf_list=[]; jb_list=[]
  for p in range(n):
    s_low = min(-p, p - n + 1)
    s_high = max(n - 1 - p, p)
    s = np.arange(s_low, s_high + 1, dtype=np.int32)
    m = n - 1 + s
    jf = p + s
    jb = p - s
    ms_list.append(m.astype(np.int32))
    jf_list.append(jf.astype(np.int32))
    jb_list.append(jb.astype(np.int32))
  return ms_list,jf_list,jb_list
```

def coord_step(c,h,p,n,prep,max_step,bis):
  ms,jf,jb,diag = prep[p]
  h0=h[p]
  L = len(ms)
  a = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
  vf = (jf >=0) & (jf < n)
  if np.any(vf): a[vf] += 1.0 - h[jf[vf]]
  jb = ... # jb from prep
  vb = (jb >=0) & (jb < n)
  if np.any(vb): a[vb] -= h[jb[vb]]
  ad = a[diag]
  a[diag] = 0.0
  pos = a > 1e-14
  neg = a < -1e-14
  zero = (a <= 1e-14) & (a >= -1e-14)
  zero[diag] = False  # but if diag already a=0; zero length L
  bc = c[ms] - a*h0
  zero_mask_c = bc[zero] if np.any(zero)
  dom_lo=...; dom_hi=...
  lo=0.0; hi=float(np.max(c)) + 1e-12
  # Optional lower bound? skip.
  for _ in range(bis):
     mid=(lo+hi)*0.5
     xlo=dom_lo; xhi=dom_hi; feasible=True
     if np.any(zero) and float(np.max(bc[zero])) > mid + 1e-12: feasible=False
     if feasible and np.any(pos):
        xhi = min(xhi, float(np.min((mid - bc[pos]) / a[pos])))
     if feasible and np.any(neg):
        xlo = max(xlo, float(np.max((mid - bc[neg]) / a[neg])))
     if feasible:
        Cq = mid - c[ms[diag]] + h0 - h0*h0
        if Cq < -1e-12: feasible=False
        elif Cq < 0.25 - 1e-12:
           disc=1.0-4.0*Cq
           if disc<0: disc=0
           r1=(1.0-np.sqrt(disc))*0.5
           r2=(1.0+np.sqrt(disc))*0.5
           left = (dom_lo, min(dom_hi, r1))
           right = (max(dom_lo, r2), dom_hi)
           ok=False
           # Use current xlo,xhi after affine
           if xlo <= left[1] and left[1] >= dom_lo:
               # interval overlap with affine? Need overlap of [xlo,xhi] and [dom_lo, left[1]]
               if max(xlo,dom_lo) <= min(xhi,left[1]) +1e-12: ok=True
           if not ok and xhi >= right[0] and max(xlo,right[0]) <= min(xhi,dom_hi)+1e-12: ok=True
           # simpler: after affine interval [xlo,xhi] (clamped), check if overlaps allowed
           # We'll implement clean after computing.
```

Let's implement top-level:
```
def subgrad_scaled(h,n,c,k,beta):
  dx=2.0/n
  if len(c)<=k: idx=np.arange(len(c)); vals=c
  else: idx=np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]; vals=c[idx]
  z=np.exp(beta*(vals-vals.max())); w=z/z.sum()
  g=np.zeros(n)
  for ii,ww in zip(idx,w):
     s=int(ii)-(n-1)
     if s>=0:
        if n-s>0: g[:n-s] += ww*(1.0 - h[s:n])
        if n-s>0: g[s:] -= ww*h[:n-s]
     else:
        t=-s
        if n-t>0:
           g[t:] += ww*(1.0 - h[:n-t])
           g[:n-t] -= ww*h[t:]
  return dx*g
```
For s=0: first g+=1-h; second g-=h =>1-2h. Good.

Line search with gradient of raw? We use c scaled in weights, return dx*g. Good.

`project_feasible` exact sum. Use n maybe int. If n==0? no.
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    target = 0.5 * n
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(300):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = room.sum()
            if tot <= 1e-14:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * room
        else:
            tot = h.sum()
            if tot <= 1e-14:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * h
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
        frac = np.where((h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12))[0]
        if frac.size:
            order = frac[np.argsort(np.minimum(h[frac], 1.0 - h[frac]))]
            for idx in order:
                step = min(max(diff, -h[idx]), 1.0 - h[idx])
                h[idx] += step
                diff -= step
                if abs(diff) < 1e-12: break
    return h
```

    if diff>0: order=np.where(h<1)[0]

    for idx in order: ...

```
def get_prep(n):
    if n not in _PREP_CACHE:
       ms=[]; jf=[]; jb=[]; diag=[]
       for p in range(n):
           sl = min(-p, p-n+1)
          sh = max(n-1-p, p)
           s = np.arange(sl, sh+1, dtype=np.int32)
           m = (n-1 + s).astype(np.int32)
           jf = (p + s).astype(np.int32)
           jb = (p - s).astype(np.int32)
           ms.append(m); jf.append(jf); jb.append(jb); diag.append(int(-sl))
       _PREP_CACHE[n]=(tuple(ms), tuple(jf), tuple(jb), tuple(diag))
    return _PREP_CACHE[n]
```

`coordinate_descent`:
```
def coordinate_descent(h,n,wall,max_step,bis,max_passes=10000):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
    best_c=c5_raw(c,n)
    best_h=h.copy()
    if wall <=0: return best_h,best_c
    t0=time.time(); t_end=t0+wall
    prep=get_prep(n)
    ms_t,jf_t,jb_t,diag_t=prep
    B=max(10, n//20)
    passes=0
    while time.time() < t_end and passes < max_passes:
       order=np.arange(n)
       if passes % 2 ==1: order=order[::-1]
       improved=False
       # maybe every 3 passes random shuffle? Need rng; not passed. Use deterministic pattern to avoid cycles: pass%3 rotate? Could use order = np.roll(order, (passes*n//max(1,n))? No rng.
       for idx,p in enumerate(order):
          if idx % B == 0 and idx > 0:
             h=project_feasible(h,n)
             c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
             cur=c5_raw(c,n)
             if cur < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c=cur; best_h=h.copy()
             if time.time() >= t_end: break
          if time.time() >= t_end: break
          x,a=coord_step(c,h,p,n,prep,max_step,bis)
          if abs(x - h[p]) > 1e-10:
             delta=x-h[p]
             h[p]=x
             c[p? ms] += delta*a
             c[ms_t[p][diag_t[p]]] -= delta*delta
             improved=True
       h=project_feasible(h,n)
       c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
       cur=c5_raw(c,n)
       if cur < best_c:
          best_c=cur; best_h=h.copy()
       if not improved:
          break
       passes +=1
    return best_h,best_c
```
But `ms_t[p]` and `diag_t[p]` in update; correct. If coord_step returns a length L; c[ms] update. `ms_t[p][diag_t[p]]` is center index. Good.

Potential: In periodic projection, h changed, but local `ms_t` still. Good.

`gradient_descent`:
Need `c` scaled for weights? We can use raw c for weights and compute gradient raw coefficients then scale step? Let's use scaled c to keep step scale. `c_scaled = np.correlate(h,1-h)*dx`.
```
def gradient_descent(h,n,wall,k=60,beta=150.0):
   h=project_feasible(h,n)
   dx=2.0/n
   c= np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full')*dx
   best_c=c.max()
   best_h=h.copy()
   t_end=...
   step=0.05
   stall=0
   while time.time()<t_end:
      g=subgrad_scaled(h,n,c,k,beta)
      gn=np.linalg.norm(g)
      if gn<1e-8: break
      cur=best_c
      acc=False
      st=step
      for _ in range(12):
         hp=project_feasible(h - st*g, n)
         hp_c=c5(hp,n)
         if hp_c < cur - 1e-10:
            h=hp; c=np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full')*dx
            cur=hp_c
            if cur < best_c: best_c=cur; best_h=h.copy(); stall=0
            acc=True; step=min(0.1, st*1.2?)
            break
         st*=0.5
      if not acc:
         step=max(1e-4, step*0.5)
         stall +=1
         if stall>5 or step<1e-4: break
   return best_h,best_c
```
Need avoid reusing h at current if best_h differs? We maintain h as current; if not improve break. If best_h updated, continue from best_h? If a bad step but then no accept, break. Good.
Step scaling: If accept with st, maybe increase `step=max(step, st*1.5)` up to 0.1. Use constant.

def c5_from_raw(raw,n): return float(np.max(raw)*(2.0/n))
```

def refine(h,n,wall,quick=False):
  if wall <=1: return h,c5(h,n)
  h=project_feasible(h,n)
  cur=c5(h,n)
  if wall > 30 and not quick:
    h2,c2=gradient_descent(h,n,min(20,0.2*wall), k=50,beta=150)
    if c2 < cur: h,cur=h2,c2
  # coordinate descent
  max_step = 0.05 if quick else 0.08
  bis = 22 if quick else 28
  h2,c2=coordinate_descent(h,n,wall-0.1,max_step,bis)
  return h2,c2
```

`interpolate_h`:
```
def interpolate_h(old,n):
 old=np.asarray(old,float)
 if len(old)==n: h=old.copy()
 else:
  x_old=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,len(old),endpoint=False)
  x_new=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n,endpoint=False)
  h=np.interp(x_new,x_old,old)
 return project_feasible(h,n)
```

`make_binary`:
```
def make_binary(h,n):
 h=np.asarray(h,float)
 if n % 2 !=0:
   return project_feasible((h>0.5).astype(float),n)
 target=n//2
 bh=(h>0.5).astype(float)
 cnt=int(bh.sum())
 if cnt > target:
    idx1=np.where(bh==1.0)[0]
    if idx1.size:
      order=idx1[np.argsort(h[idx1])]
      bh[order[:cnt-target]]=0.0
 elif cnt < target:
    idx0=np.where(bh==0.0)[0]
    if idx0.size:
      order=idx0[np.argsort(h[idx0])][::-1]
      bh[order[:target-cnt]]=1.0
 return bh
```
For even sum exact.

`block_start`:
```
def block_start(n,rng):
 k=int(rng.randint(4,16))
 h=np.zeros(n)
 pos=0; val=rng.randint(2)
 lengths=rng.randint(1, max(2,n//k+2), size=k)
 for length in lengths:
   if pos>=n: break
   take=min(length,n-pos)
   h[pos:pos+take]=val
   pos+=take
   val ^= 1
 # ensure balanced via make_binary; if h all zero maybe
 return make_binary(h,n)
```
`trig_start`:
```
def trig_start(n,rng):
 x=np.linspace(0,2,n,endpoint=False)
 h=np.full(n,0.5)
 for _ in range(int(rng.randint(2,5))):
    f=float(rng.uniform(0.5,3.0))
    ph=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
    amp=float(rng.uniform(0.2,0.45))
    if rng.rand()<0.5: h += amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*f*x+ph)
    else: h += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*x+ph)
 return project_feasible(np.clip(h,0,1),n)
```
Maybe low frequency f ~1 better than high. Use f uniform 0.25,1.5? If high, random. Use f in [0.5,2.0]. Good.

Thus in `coord_step`, for a given m, s = n-1 - m (not m-(n-1)). Affected coefficient for variable p:
- if p is h_i: i=p, j=p+s, condition p+s valid -> +(1 - h[p+s]).

```
import numpy as np
import time

_PREP_CACHE = {}

def get_prep(n):
    if n not in _PREP_CACHE:
       ms=[]; jf=[]; jb=[]; diag=[]
       for p in range(n):
          sl = min(-p, p - n + 1)
          sh = max(n - 1 - p, p)
          s = np.arange(sl, sh + 1, dtype=np.int32)
          m = (n - 1 - s).astype(np.int32)
          jf_arr = (p + s).astype(np.int32)
          jb_arr = (p - s).astype(np.int32)
          ms.append(m); jf.append(jf_arr); jb.append(jb_arr); diag.append(int(-sl))
       _PREP_CACHE[n] = (tuple(ms), tuple(jf), tuple(jb), tuple(diag))
    return _PREP_CACHE[n]
```
For p=0,n=700, sl=-699, sh=699, length1399; diag=699. m[699]=0? For s=0 at index699, m=699 correct. Good.

Project:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(200):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13: break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h; tot=room.sum()
            if tot <= 1e-14: break
            h = h + (diff/tot)*room
        else:
            tot=h.sum()
            if tot <=1e-14: break
            h = h + (diff/tot)*h
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
        frac=np.where((h>1e-12)&(h<1-1e-12))[0]
        if frac.size:
           order=frac[np.argsort(np.minimum(h[frac], 1.0-h[frac]))]
           for idx in order:
              step = min(max(diff, -h[idx]), 1.0-h[idx])
              h[idx]+=step; diff-=step
              if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
        else:
           order=np.where(h<1 if diff>0 else h>0)[0]? Syntax.
```
Need syntax for `np.where(h < 1.0 if diff > 0 else h > 0.0)[0]` is bad. Do:
```
           if diff > 0:
              order = np.where(h < 1.0)[0]
           else:
              order = np.where(h > 0.0)[0]
           for idx in order:
              step = min(max(diff, -h[idx]), 1.0 - h[idx])
              h[idx] += step; diff -= step
              if abs(diff) < 1e-12: break
```

`subgrad_scaled`:
```
def subgrad_scaled(h,n,c,k,beta):
    dx=2.0/n
    c = np.asarray(c)
    kk = int(min(k, len(c)))
    if kk <=0: return np.zeros(n)
    if kk == len(c): idx=np.arange(len(c))
    else: idx=np.argpartition(c, -kk)[-kk:]
    vals=c[idx]
    z=np.exp(beta*(vals-vals.max()))
    w=z/z.sum()
    g=np.zeros(n)
    for ii, ww in zip(idx, w):
       s = n - 1 - int(ii)
       if s >=0:
          L=n-s
          if L>0:
             g[:L] += ww*(1.0 - h[s:])
             g[s:] -= ww*h[:L]
       else:
          t=-s
          L=n-t
          if L>0:
             g[t:] += ww*(1.0 - h[:L])
             g[:L] -= ww*h[t:]
    return dx*g
```
Check s>=0 L=n-s. `h[s:]` length L. `g[:L]` first. `g[s:]` length L, `h[:L]` length L. Good. s<0 t, L=n-t. `h[:L]` length L, `g[t:]` length L. second `g[:L]`, `h[t:]` length L. Good.

`gradient_descent`:
```
def gradient_descent(h,n,wall,k=60,beta=150):
    t0=time.time(); t_end=t0+max(0,wall)
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    dx=2.0/n
    c=np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full')*dx
    best_c=float(c.max()); best_h=h.copy()
    step=0.03; stall=0; it=0
    while time.time()<t_end and it<100:
       it+=1
       g=subgrad_scaled(h,n,c,k,beta)
       if np.linalg.norm(g) < 1e-8: break
       cur=best_c
       acc=False
       st=step
       for _ in range(8):
          hp=project_feasible(h - st*g, n)
          hp_c=c5(hp,n)
          if hp_c < cur - 1e-9:
             h=hp; c=np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full')*dx
             cur=hp_c
             if cur < best_c:
                best_c=cur; best_h=h.copy(); stall=0
             acc=True
             step=min(0.08, max(step, st*1.5))
             break
          st*=0.5
       if not acc:
          step *=0.5; stall+=1
          if stall>4 or step<1e-4: break
    return best_h,best_c
```
Step increase maybe after accept with reduced st. If accept with st small, step may remain previous. `max(step, st*1.5)` can increase beyond previous? acceptable. If previous 0.03, st maybe0.00077 if reductions, no increase. Good.

def refine(h,n,wall,max_step=None,bis=None):
    wall=float(max(0.0, wall))
    if wall <=0:
       h=project_feasible(h,n); return h,c5(h,n)
    t0=time.time()
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    cur=c5(h,n)
    if wall > 40:
       gw=min(20.0, 0.2*wall)
       h2,c2=gradient_descent(h,n,gw)
       if c2 < cur:
          h,cur=h2,c2
    rem=wall-(time.time()-t0)
    if rem <=0:
       return h, cur
    if max_step is None:
       max_step=0.1 if rem>60 else 0.07
    if bis is None:
       bis=30 if rem>60 else 24
    h2,c2=coordinate_descent(h,n,rem,max_step,bis)
    return h2,c2
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time

_PREP_CACHE = {}


def get_prep(n):
    if n not in _PREP_CACHE:
        ms = []
        jf = []
        jb = []
        diag = []
        for p in range(n):
            sl = min(-p, p - n + 1)
            sh = max(n - 1 - p, p)
            s = np.arange(sl, sh + 1, dtype=np.int32)
            m = (n - 1 - s).astype(np.int32)
            jf_arr = (p + s).astype(np.int32)
            jb_arr = (p - s).astype(np.int32)
            ms.append(m)
            jf.append(jf_arr)
            jb.append(jb_arr)
            diag.append(int(-sl))
        _PREP_CACHE[n] = (tuple(ms), tuple(jf), tuple(jb), tuple(diag))
    return _PREP_CACHE[n]


def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(200):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = room.sum()
            if tot <= 1e-14:
                break
            h = h + (diff / tot) * room
        else:
            tot = h.sum()
            if tot <= 1e-14:
                break
            h = h + (diff / tot) * h
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
        frac = np.where((h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12))[0]
        if frac.size:
            order = frac[np.argsort(np.minimum(h[frac], 1.0 - h[frac]))]
            for idx in order:
                step = min(max(diff, -h[idx]), 1.0 - h[idx])
                h[idx] += step
                diff -= step
                if abs(diff) < 1e-12:
                    break
        else:
            if diff > 0:
                order = np.where(h < 1.0)[0]
            else:
                order = np.where(h > 0.0)[0]
            for idx in order:
                step = min(max(diff, -h[idx]), 1.0 - h[idx])
                h[idx] += step
                diff -= step
                if abs(diff) < 1e-12:
                    break
    return h


def raw_corr(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")


def c5(h, n):
    return float(np.max(raw_corr(h)) * (2.0 / n))


def interval_overlap(lo1, hi1, lo2, hi2):
    return max(lo1, lo2) <= min(hi1, hi2) + 1e-12


def coord_step(c, h, p, n, prep, max_step, bis):
    ms = prep[0][p]
    jf = prep[1][p]
    jb = prep[2][p]
    diag = prep[3][p]

    h0 = h[p]
    a = np.zeros(len(ms), dtype=float)

    vf = (jf >= 0) & (jf < n)
    if np.any(vf):
        a[vf] += 1.0 - h[jf[vf]]
    vb = (jb >= 0) & (jb < n)
    if np.any(vb):
        a[vb] -= h[jb[vb]]

    ad = a[diag]
    a[diag] = 0.0

    pos = a > 1e-14
    neg = a < -1e-14
    zero = (a <= 1e-14) & (a >= -1e-14)
    zero[diag] = False

    any_pos = bool(np.any(pos))
    any_neg = bool(np.any(neg))
    any_zero = bool(np.any(zero))
    zero_bc_max = float(np.max(bc[zero])) if any_zero else -np.inf

    bc = c[ms] - a * h0
    c0 = c[ms[diag]]

    dlo = max(0.0, h0 - max_step)
    dhi = min(1.0, h0 + max_step)
    if dhi - dlo <= 1e-14:
        a[diag] = ad
        return h0, a

    m_min = int(ms[-1])
    m_max = int(ms[0])
    lo = 0.0
    if m_min > 0:
        lo = max(lo, float(np.max(c[:m_min])))
    if m_max < c.size - 1:
        lo = max(lo, float(np.max(c[m_max + 1:])))
    hi = float(np.max(c)) + 1e-12

    if hi - lo < 1e-12:
        a[diag] = ad
        return h0, a

    for _ in range(bis):
        if hi - lo < 1e-13:
            break
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        xlo = dlo
        xhi = dhi
        feasible = True

        if any_zero and zero_bc_max > mid + 1e-12:
            feasible = False

        if feasible and any_pos:
            xhi = min(xhi, float(np.min((mid - bc[pos]) / a[pos])))
        if feasible and any_neg:
            xlo = max(xlo, float(np.max((mid - bc[neg]) / a[neg])))

        if feasible and xlo > xhi + 1e-12:
            feasible = False

        if feasible:
            xlo = max(xlo, dlo)
            xhi = min(xhi, dhi)
            Cq = mid - c0 + h0 - h0 * h0
            if Cq < -1e-12:
                feasible = False
            elif Cq < 0.25 - 1e-12:
                disc = 1.0 - 4.0 * Cq
                if disc < 0.0:
                    disc = 0.0
                r1 = 0.5 * (1.0 - np.sqrt(disc))
                r2 = 0.5 * (1.0 + np.sqrt(disc))
                ok = interval_overlap(xlo, xhi, dlo, min(r1, dhi))
                if not ok:
                    ok = interval_overlap(xlo, xhi, max(r2, dlo), dhi)
                if not ok:
                    feasible = False

        if feasible:
            hi = mid
        else:
            lo = mid

    L = hi
    xlo = dlo
    xhi = dhi
    if any_pos:
        xhi = min(xhi, float(np.min((L - bc[pos]) / a[pos])))
    if any_neg:
        xlo = max(xlo, float(np.max((L - bc[neg]) / a[neg])))
    xlo = max(xlo, dlo)
    xhi = min(xhi, dhi)

    Cq = L - c0 + h0 - h0 * h0
    intervals = []
    if Cq >= 0.25 - 1e-12:
        if xhi >= xlo - 1e-12:
            intervals.append((xlo, xhi))
    else:
        if Cq < -1e-12:
            Cq = 0.0
        disc = 1.0 - 4.0 * Cq
        if disc < 0.0:
            disc = 0.0
        r1 = 0.5 * (1.0 - np.sqrt(disc))
        r2 = 0.5 * (1.0 + np.sqrt(disc))
        lo1 = max(xlo, dlo)
        hi1 = min(xhi, min(r1, dhi))
        if hi1 >= lo1 - 1e-12:
            intervals.append((lo1, hi1))
        lo2 = max(xlo, max(r2, dlo))
        hi2 = min(xhi, dhi)
        if hi2 >= lo2 - 1e-12:
            intervals.append((lo2, hi2))

    best_x = h0
    best_d = 1e100
    for llo, hhi in intervals:
        xopt = llo
        if h0 < llo:
            xopt = llo
        elif h0 > hhi:
            xopt = hhi
        else:
            xopt = h0
        d = abs(xopt - h0)
        if d < best_d:
            best_d = d
            best_x = xopt

    x = float(np.clip(best_x, 0.0, 1.0))
    a[diag] = ad
    return x, a


def coordinate_descent(h, n, wall, max_step, bis):
    t0 = time.time()
    t_end = t0 + max(0.0, wall)

    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c = raw_corr(h)
    best_c = float(np.max(c) * (2.0 / n))
    best_h = h.copy()

    if wall <= 0.0:
        return best_h, best_c

    prep = get_prep(n)
    B = max(20, n // 10)
    plateau = 0
    passes = 0
    max_passes = 10000

    while time.time() < t_end and passes < max_passes:
        order = np.arange(n)
        if passes % 2 == 1:
            order = order[::-1]

        improved = False
        for idx, p in enumerate(order):
            if idx and idx % B == 0:
                h = project_feasible(h, n)
                c = raw_corr(h)
                cur = float(np.max(c) * (2.0 / n))
                if cur < best_c - 1e-12:
                    best_c = cur
                    best_h = h.copy()
                if time.time() >= t_end:
                    break
            if time.time() >= t_end:
                break

            x, a = coord_step(c, h, p, n, prep, max_step, bis)
            if abs(x - h[p]) > 1e-10:
                delta = x - h[p]
                mst = prep[0][p]
                h[p] = x
                c[mst] += delta * a
                c[mst[prep[3][p]]] -= delta * delta
                improved = True

        h = project_feasible(h, n)
        c = raw_corr(h)
        cur = float(np.max(c) * (2.0 / n))
        if cur < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_c = cur
            best_h = h.copy()
            plateau = 0
        else:
            plateau += 1
            if cur > best_c + 1e-7:
                h = best_h.copy()
                c = raw_corr(h)
                plateau += 1

        if (not improved) or plateau >= 4:
            break
        passes += 1

    return best_h, best_c


def subgrad_scaled(h, n, c, k, beta):
    dx = 2.0 / n
    kk = int(min(k, len(c)))
    if kk <= 0:
        return np.zeros(n)
    if kk == len(c):
        idx = np.arange(len(c))
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(c, -kk)[-kk:]
    vals = c[idx]
    z = np.exp(beta * (vals - vals.max()))
    w = z / z.sum()
    g = np.zeros(n)
    for ii, ww in zip(idx, w):
        s = n - 1 - int(ii)
        if s >= 0:
            L = n - s
            if L > 0:
                g[:L] += ww * (1.0 - h[s:])
                g[s:] -= ww * h[:L]
        else:
            t = -s
            L = n - t
            if L > 0:
                g[t:] += ww * (1.0 - h[:L])
                g[:L] -= ww * h[t:]
    return dx * g


def gradient_descent(h, n, wall, k=60, beta=150.0):
    t0 = time.time()
    t_end = t0 + max(0.0, wall)
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    c = raw_corr(h) * dx
    best_c = float(c.max())
    best_h = h.copy()

    step = 0.03
    stall = 0
    it = 0
    while time.time() < t_end and it < 100:
        it += 1
        g = subgrad_scaled(h, n, c, k, beta)
        if np.linalg.norm(g) < 1e-8:
            break
        cur = best_c
        acc = False
        st = step
        for _ in range(8):
            hp = project_feasible(h - st * g, n)
            hp_c = c5(hp, n)
            if hp_c < cur - 1e-9:
                h = hp
                c = raw_corr(h) * dx
                cur = hp_c
                if cur < best_c:
                    best_c = cur
                    best_h = h.copy()
                    stall = 0
                acc = True
                step = min(0.08, max(step, st * 1.5))
                break
            st *= 0.5
        if not acc:
            step *= 0.5
            stall += 1
            if stall > 4 or step < 1e-4:
                break
    return best_h, best_c


def interpolate_h(old, n):
    old = np.asarray(old, dtype=float)
    if len(old) == n:
        h = old.copy()
    else:
        x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, len(old), endpoint=False)
        x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
        h = np.interp(x_new, x_old, old)
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def make_binary(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if n % 2 != 0:
        return project_feasible((h > 0.5).astype(float), n)
    target = n // 2
    bh = (h > 0.5).astype(float)
    cnt = int(bh.sum())
    if cnt > target:
        idx1 = np.where(bh == 1.0)[0]
        if idx1.size:
            order = idx1[np.argsort(h[idx1])]
            bh[order[:cnt - target]] = 0.0
    elif cnt < target:
        idx0 = np.where(bh == 0.0)[0]
        if idx0.size:
            order = idx0[np.argsort(h[idx0])][::-1]
            bh[order[:target - cnt]] = 1.0
    return bh


def block_start(n, rng):
    k = int(rng.randint(4, 16))
    h = np.zeros(n)
    pos = 0
    val = int(rng.randint(2))
    sizes = rng.randint(1, max(2, n // k + 3), size=k)
    for length in sizes:
        if pos >= n:
            break
        take = min(int(length), n - pos)
        h[pos:pos + take] = val
        pos += take
        val ^= 1
    return make_binary(h, n)


def trig_start(n, rng):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
    h = np.full(n, 0.5)
    for _ in range(int(rng.randint(2, 5))):
        f = float(rng.uniform(0.25, 2.0))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.20, 0.45))
        if rng.rand() < 0.5:
            h += amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + ph)
        else:
            h += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + ph)
    return project_feasible(np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0), n)


def refine(h, n, wall, max_step=None, bis=None):
    wall = float(max(0.0, wall))
    if wall <= 0.0:
        h = project_feasible(h, n)
        return h, c5(h, n)

    t0 = time.time()
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    cur = c5(h, n)

    if wall > 40.0:
        gw = min(20.0, 0.2 * wall)
        h2, c2 = gradient_descent(h, n, gw)
        if c2 < cur:
            h, cur = h2, c2

    rem = wall - (time.time() - t0)
    if rem <= 0.0:
        return h, cur

    if max_step is None:
        max_step = 0.10 if rem > 60.0 else 0.07
    if bis is None:
        bis = 30 if rem > 60.0 else 24

    h2, c2 = coordinate_descent(h, n, rem, max_step, bis)
    return h2, c2


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    budget_s = max(1, int(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + budget_s - 10

    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n0 = int(len(h0))
    except Exception:
        h0 = None
        n0 = 700

    if h0 is None or n0 <= 0:
        n0 = 700
        h0 = np.full(n0, 0.5)

    if n0 > 1000:
        n0 = 950
        h0 = interpolate_h(h0, n0)

    h0 = project_feasible(h0, n0)
    best_h = h0.copy()
    best_n = n0
    best_c = c5(best_h, best_n)

    rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.time())
    if rem > 60.0:
        wall = min(220.0, 0.4 * rem)
        h2, c2 = refine(best_h, best_n, wall, max_step=0.10, bis=30)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, best_n = h2, c2, best_n

    base = best_n
    ns = []
    for val in (base, base - 100, base - 50, base + 50, base + 100,
                300, 400, 500, 600, 700, 800, 900, 999):
        if 200 <= val <= 1000 and val not in ns:
            ns.append(int(val))

    sweep_end = deadline - 180.0
    for ntry in ns:
        if time.time() > sweep_end:
            break
        htry = interpolate_h(best_h, ntry)
        ctry = c5(htry, ntry)
        if ctry <= best_c + 0.05:
            factor = (700.0 / max(1, ntry)) ** 2
            wall = 30.0 * factor
            max_wall = max(8.0, (deadline - time.time() - 120.0) * 0.30)
            wall = min(wall, max_wall)
            if wall >= 8.0:
                quick = wall < 20.0
                h2, c2 = refine(htry, ntry, wall,
                                max_step=0.07 if quick else 0.09,
                                bis=22 if quick else 28)
                if c2 < best_c:
                    best_h, best_c, best_n = h2, c2, ntry

    start_ns = []
    for val in (best_n, max(300, min(900, best_n - 100)),
                max(300, min(900, best_n + 100)), 500, 700):
        if 250 <= val <= 1000 and int(val) not in start_ns:
            start_ns.append(int(val))
    if not start_ns:
        start_ns = [600]

    random_end = deadline - 120.0
    for it in range(8):
        if time.time() > random_end:
            break
        nt = start_ns[it % len(start_ns)]
        if rng.rand() < 0.5:
            htry = block_start(nt, rng)
        else:
            htry = trig_start(nt, rng)
        ctry = c5(htry, nt)
        if ctry <= best_c + 0.12:
            factor = (700.0 / max(1, nt)) ** 2
            wall = 20.0 * factor
            wall = min(wall, max(8.0, (deadline - time.time() - 60.0) * 0.25))
            if wall >= 8.0:
                h2, c2 = refine(htry, nt, wall, max_step=0.07, bis=22)
                if c2 < best_c:
                    best_h, best_c, best_n = h2, c2, nt

    sigmas = [0.02, 0.04, 0.07, 0.10, 0.15, 0.22]
    pcount = 0
    perturb_end = deadline - 60.0
    while time.time() < perturb_end and pcount < 14:
        sg = sigmas[min(pcount, len(sigmas) - 1)]
        hp = project_feasible(best_h + rng.normal(0.0, sg, len(best_h)), best_n)
        if pcount % 5 == 4 and best_n % 2 == 0:
            hp = make_binary(hp, best_n)

        factor = (700.0 / max(1, best_n)) ** 2
        wall = 18.0 * factor
        wall = min(wall, max(6.0, (deadline - time.time() - 30.0) * 0.30))
        if wall >= 6.0:
            h2, c2 = refine(hp, best_n, wall, max_step=0.06, bis=22)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = h2, c2
        pcount += 1

    rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.time() - 2.0)
    if rem > 4.0:
        h2, c2 = refine(best_h, best_n, rem, max_step=0.06, bis=32)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = h2, c2

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    best_c = c5(best_h, best_n)
    return best_h, float(best_c), int(len(best_h))
```
